Title: Lettres de Monsieur Fléchier, évêque de Nismes, et l'un des quarante de l'Académie Françoise, avec quelques autres pièces de l'auteur qui ne se trouvent pas dans l'édition de Paris
Creator: Esprit Fléchier
Publisher: La Haye: chez Jean van Duren, marchand libraire
Publication Date: 1712
Rights: Public Domain (published 1712, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'NOT_IN_COPYRIGHT')
Language: fra
Source: Internet Archive item lettresdemonsie00flgoog (https://archive.org/details/lettresdemonsie00flgoog), the item's own lettresdemonsie00flgoog_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Oxford University; host metadata date: 1712. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: e10c44dd6c62f9f4da6af6815957d717ec67fc25d6d19861e8e02260c151c2a6.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; the host's front matter up to and including the line beginning 'des recherches en ligne' removed (111 lines); nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Google Books scan from Oxford University; the Google notice at the head of the file is removed as recorded above. Fair OCR with the long s read as f and some pages roughly read.
Content note: The letters of the bishop of Nîmes, many written during the Camisard war, in which he reports the 'fanatiques' and their burnings and killings in his diocese, with his Academy eulogy prefixed.
